DHCP Options in YANG

Abstract

This memo specifies the process for creating YANG versions of DHCP server options. By providing the appropriate data model, automation of both YANG-based management systems and DHCP systems can be improved.¶

About This Document

This note is to be removed before publishing as an RFC.¶

The latest revision of this draft can be found at https://elear.github.io/draft-lear-yangifying-dhcp-opts/draft-lear-yangifying-dhcp-opts.html. Status information for this document may be found at https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/draft-lear-yangifying-dhcp-opts/.¶

Source for this draft and an issue tracker can be found at https://github.com/elear/draft-lear-yangifying-dhcp-opts.¶

Status of This Memo

This Internet-Draft is submitted in full conformance with the provisions of BCP 78 and BCP 79.¶

Internet-Drafts are working documents of the Internet Engineering Task Force (IETF). Note that other groups may also distribute working documents as Internet-Drafts. The list of current Internet-Drafts is at https://datatracker.ietf.org/drafts/current/.¶

Internet-Drafts are draft documents valid for a maximum of six months and may be updated, replaced, or obsoleted by other documents at any time. It is inappropriate to use Internet-Drafts as reference material or to cite them other than as "work in progress."¶

This Internet-Draft will expire on 27 May 2027.¶

1. Introduction

DHCP options contain whole lot of Cool Stuff that clients might like to know about their current environment. The hint is in the name as to how this information has been transported: DHCP. But those options can be used in other ways, either to configure clients through other substrates or even as part of a broader management framework to configure DHCP servers themselves.¶

For example, if the inforamtion is properly structured it could be included at the EAP layer as part of TEAP /* Reference here */¶

[RFC9243] defines the base YANG models for DHCPv6 servers, relays and clients, and includes YANG schema definitions for configuring the DHCPv6 options that are defined in [RFC9915]. [RFC9243], Appendix B describes rules and a general approach for extending the base YANG models with configuration nodes for additional DHCPv6 options. This memo follows these rule and approach, and remains focused on DHCPv6.¶

DHCP has been around a very long time, since the 1990s, and its predecessor BOOTP may have co-existed with dinosaurs. The implication of this is that options specifications were strictly narrative alongside a packet diagram. The specification of the option contents themselves was not governed by a former data model. This draft changes how new options get defined so that YANG is now used to defined the option contents. For this reason it updates /* reference here */.¶

Finally, we provide an initial registry of server options that have already been defined. For the sake of sanity, we have not attempted to go beyond simple typing found in /* reference here */.¶

i# DHCPv6 Option Models¶

1.1. RFC 3319: DHCPv6 options model ietf-dhcpv6-opt-sip

This model covers the DHCPv6 options specified in [RFC3319]: Dynamic Host Configuration Protocol (DHCPv6) Options for Session Initiation Protocol (SIP) Servers.¶

Table 1
IANA option code IANA option name
21 OPTION_SIP_SERVER_D
22 OPTION_SIP_SERVER_A 
module: ietf-dhcpv6-opt-sip
  augment /dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/dhc6-srv:option-sets/
         dhc6-srv:option-set:
    +--rw sip-server-domain-name-list-option!
       +--rw sip-server-domain-name* inet:domain-name
    +--rw sip-server-address-list-option!
       +--rw sip-server-address* inet:ipv6-address
¶ 
<CODE BEGINS> file "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-sip.yang"

module ietf-dhcpv6-opt-sip {
  yang-version 1.1;
  namespace "urn:ietf:params:xml:ns:yang:"
    + "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-sip";
  prefix sip;

  import ietf-inet-types {
    prefix inet;
    reference
      "RFC 6991: Common YANG Data Types";
  }
  import ietf-dhcpv6-server {
    prefix dhc6-srv;
    reference
      "RFC 9243: A YANG Data Model for DHCPv6
       Configuration";
  }

  organization
    "IETF Dynamic Host Configuration (DHC) Working Group";
  contact
    "DHC Working Group
     WG Web: <https://datatracker.ietf.org/wg/dhc/>
     WG List: <mailto:dhcwg@ietf.org>";
  description
     "This module defines the DHCPv6 options from RFC 3319: Dynamic
      Host Configuration Protocol (DHCPv6) Options for Session
      Initiation Protocol (SIP) Servers.";
  reference "RFC 3319: Dynamic Host Configuration Protocol (DHCPv6)
    Options for Session Initiation Protocol (SIP) Servers";

  revision 2026-11-19 {
    description "Initial revision.";
    reference "RFC 3319: Dynamic Host Configuration Protocol (DHCPv6)
      Options for Session Initiation Protocol (SIP) Servers";
  }

  grouping sip-server-domain-name-list-option-group {
    description "OPTION_SIP_SERVER_D (21) SIP Servers Domain-Name
      List.";
    reference "RFC 3319: Dynamic Host Configuration Protocol (DHCPv6)
      Options for Session Initiation Protocol (SIP) Servers";
    container sip-server-domain-name-list-option {
      presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
      description "OPTION_SIP_SERVER_D (21) SIP Servers Domain-Name
        List option.";
      leaf-list sip-server-domain-name {
        type inet:domain-name;
        description "SIP server domain name.";
      }
    }
  }

  grouping sip-server-address-list-option-group {
    description "OPTION_SIP_SERVER_A (22) SIP Servers IPv6 Address
      List.";
    reference "RFC 3319: Dynamic Host Configuration Protocol (DHCPv6)
      Options for Session Initiation Protocol (SIP) Servers";
    container sip-server-address-list-option {
      presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
      description "OPTION_SIP_SERVER_A (22) SIP Servers IPv6 Address
        List option.";
      leaf-list sip-server-address {
        type inet:ipv6-address;
        description "SIP server IPv6 address.";
      }
    }
  }

  augment
    "/dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/"
      + "dhc6-srv:option-sets/"
      + "dhc6-srv:option-set" {
    description "Add RFC 3319 options.";
    uses sip-server-domain-name-list-option-group;
    uses sip-server-address-list-option-group;
  }

}

<CODE ENDS>
¶

1.2. RFC 3646: DHCPv6 options model ietf-dhcpv6-opt-dns

This model covers the DHCPv6 options specified in [RFC3646]: DNS Configuration options for DHCPv6.¶

Table 2
IANA option code IANA option name
23 OPTION_DNS_SERVERS
24 OPTION_DOMAIN_LIST 
module: ietf-dhcpv6-opt-dns
  augment /dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/dhc6-srv:option-sets/
         dhc6-srv:option-set:
    +--rw dns-servers-option!
       +--rw dns-server* inet:ipv6-address
    +--rw domain-list-option!
       +--rw domain-name* inet:domain-name
¶ 
<CODE BEGINS> file "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-dns.yang"

module ietf-dhcpv6-opt-dns {
  yang-version 1.1;
  namespace "urn:ietf:params:xml:ns:yang:"
    + "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-dns";
  prefix dns;

  import ietf-inet-types {
    prefix inet;
    reference
      "RFC 6991: Common YANG Data Types";
  }
  import ietf-dhcpv6-server {
    prefix dhc6-srv;
    reference
      "RFC 9243: A YANG Data Model for DHCPv6
       Configuration";
  }

  organization
    "IETF Dynamic Host Configuration (DHC) Working Group";
  contact
    "DHC Working Group
     WG Web: <https://datatracker.ietf.org/wg/dhc/>
     WG List: <mailto:dhcwg@ietf.org>";
  description
     "This module defines the DHCPv6 options from RFC 3646: DNS
      Configuration options for DHCPv6.";
  reference "RFC 3646: DNS Configuration options for DHCPv6";

  revision 2026-11-19 {
    description "Initial revision.";
    reference "RFC 3646: DNS Configuration options for DHCPv6";
  }

  grouping dns-servers-option-group {
    description "OPTION_DNS_SERVERS (23) DNS Recursive Name Server
      Option.";
    reference "RFC 3646: DNS Configuration options for DHCPv6";
    container dns-servers-option {
      presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
      description "OPTION_DNS_SERVERS (23) DNS Recursive Name Server
        Option option.";
      leaf-list dns-server {
        type inet:ipv6-address;
        description "DNS recursive name server address.";
      }
    }
  }

  grouping domain-list-option-group {
    description "OPTION_DOMAIN_LIST (24) Domain Search List Option.";
    reference "RFC 3646: DNS Configuration options for DHCPv6";
    container domain-list-option {
      presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
      description "OPTION_DOMAIN_LIST (24) Domain Search List Option
        option.";
      leaf-list domain-name {
        type inet:domain-name;
        description "Domain search name.";
      }
    }
  }

  augment
    "/dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/"
      + "dhc6-srv:option-sets/"
      + "dhc6-srv:option-set" {
    description "Add RFC 3646 options.";
    uses dns-servers-option-group;
    uses domain-list-option-group;
  }

}

<CODE ENDS>
¶

1.3. RFC 3898: DHCPv6 options model ietf-dhcpv6-opt-nis

This model covers the DHCPv6 options specified in [RFC3898]: Network Information Service (NIS) Configuration Options for DHCPv6.¶

Table 3
IANA option code IANA option name
27 OPTION_NIS_SERVERS
28 OPTION_NISP_SERVERS
29 OPTION_NIS_DOMAIN_NAME
30 OPTION_NISP_DOMAIN_NAME 
module: ietf-dhcpv6-opt-nis
  augment /dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/dhc6-srv:option-sets/
         dhc6-srv:option-set:
    +--rw nis-servers-option!
       +--rw nis-server* inet:ipv6-address
    +--rw nisp-servers-option!
       +--rw nisp-server* inet:ipv6-address
    +--rw nis-domain-name-option!
       +--rw nis-domain-name? inet:domain-name
    +--rw nisp-domain-name-option!
       +--rw nisp-domain-name? inet:domain-name
¶ 
<CODE BEGINS> file "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-nis.yang"

module ietf-dhcpv6-opt-nis {
  yang-version 1.1;
  namespace "urn:ietf:params:xml:ns:yang:"
    + "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-nis";
  prefix nis;

  import ietf-inet-types {
    prefix inet;
    reference
      "RFC 6991: Common YANG Data Types";
  }
  import ietf-dhcpv6-server {
    prefix dhc6-srv;
    reference
      "RFC 9243: A YANG Data Model for DHCPv6
       Configuration";
  }

  organization
    "IETF Dynamic Host Configuration (DHC) Working Group";
  contact
    "DHC Working Group
     WG Web: <https://datatracker.ietf.org/wg/dhc/>
     WG List: <mailto:dhcwg@ietf.org>";
  description
     "This module defines the DHCPv6 options from RFC 3898: Network
      Information Service (NIS) Configuration Options for DHCPv6.";
  reference "RFC 3898: Network Information Service (NIS) Configuration
    Options for DHCPv6";

  revision 2026-11-19 {
    description "Initial revision.";
    reference "RFC 3898: Network Information Service (NIS)
      Configuration Options for DHCPv6";
  }

  grouping nis-servers-option-group {
    description "OPTION_NIS_SERVERS (27) Network Information Service
      Servers Option.";
    reference "RFC 3898: Network Information Service (NIS)
      Configuration Options for DHCPv6";
    container nis-servers-option {
      presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
      description "OPTION_NIS_SERVERS (27) Network Information Service
        Servers Option option.";
      leaf-list nis-server {
        type inet:ipv6-address;
        description "NIS server address.";
      }
    }
  }

  grouping nisp-servers-option-group {
    description "OPTION_NISP_SERVERS (28) Network Information Service+
      Servers Option.";
    reference "RFC 3898: Network Information Service (NIS)
      Configuration Options for DHCPv6";
    container nisp-servers-option {
      presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
      description "OPTION_NISP_SERVERS (28) Network Information
        Service+ Servers Option option.";
      leaf-list nisp-server {
        type inet:ipv6-address;
        description "NIS+ server address.";
      }
    }
  }

  grouping nis-domain-name-option-group {
    description "OPTION_NIS_DOMAIN_NAME (29) Network Information
      Service Domain Name Option.";
    reference "RFC 3898: Network Information Service (NIS)
      Configuration Options for DHCPv6";
    container nis-domain-name-option {
      presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
      description "OPTION_NIS_DOMAIN_NAME (29) Network Information
        Service Domain Name Option option.";
      leaf nis-domain-name {
        type inet:domain-name;
        description "NIS domain name.";
      }
    }
  }

  grouping nisp-domain-name-option-group {

    "IETF Dynamic Host Configuration (DHC) Working Group";

     WG Web: <https://datatracker.ietf.org/wg/dhc/>
     WG List: <mailto:dhcwg@ietf.org>";

     "This module defines the DHCPv6 options from RFC 5007: DHCPv6
      Leasequery.";

    description "Initial revision.";
    reference "RFC 5007: DHCPv6 Leasequery";
  }

  grouping lq-query-option-group {

        description "Queried client DUID, when applicable.";

    }
  }

        type binary;
        description "Client DUID.";
      }
      leaf clt-time {
        type uint64;
        description "Client last-transaction time.";

      leaf ia-data {
        type binary;
        description "Identity-association data.";
      }
    }
  }

  grouping clt-time-option-group {

      Option.";

    }

      Option.";
    reference "RFC 5007: DHCPv6 Leasequery";
    container lq-relay-data-option {

        description "Peer address.";

      }

  grouping lq-client-link-option-group {

      Option.";

  }

  augment
    "/dhc6-clnt:dhcpv6-client/"
      + "dhc6-clnt:client-if/"
      + "dhc6-clnt:client-configured-options" {

    uses client-data-option-group;

      leaf major-version {
        type uint8;
        description "Major version.";
      }
      leaf minor-version {
        type uint8;
        description "Minor version.";
      }
    }
  }

  augment
    "/dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/"
      + "dhc6-srv:option-sets/"
      + "dhc6-srv:option-set" {
    description "Add RFC 5970 options.";
    uses bootfile-url-option-group;
    uses bootfile-param-option-group;
    uses client-arch-type-option-group;
    uses nii-option-group;
  }

         dhc6-srv:option-set:
    +--rw mip6-hnidf-option!
       +--rw home-network-id? inet:domain-name
    +--rw mip6-vdinf-option!
       +--rw enterprise-number? uint32
       +--rw vendor-data? binary
    +--rw mip6-idinf-option!
       +--rw identifier? binary
    +--rw mip6-udinf-option!
       +--rw user-data? binary
    +--rw mip6-hnp-option!
       +--rw home-network-prefix* inet:ipv6-prefix
    +--rw mip6-haa-option!
       +--rw home-agent-address* inet:ipv6-address
    +--rw mip6-haf-option!
       +--rw home-agent-fqdn* inet:domain-name
¶

        description "DHCPv4-over-DHCPv6 server address.";
      }
    }
  }

      + "dhc6-rly:relay-if/"

    uses dhcpv4-msg-option-group;

      description "OPTION_ADDR_REG_ENABLE (148) Address Registration
        Enable Option option.";
    }
  }

  augment
    "/dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/"
      + "dhc6-srv:option-sets/"
      + "dhc6-srv:option-set" {
    description "Add RFC 9686 options.";
    uses addr-reg-enable-option-group;
  }

}

<CODE ENDS>
¶

1.112. RFC10038: ietf-dhcpv6-opt-srv6-locator YANG Model

This model covers the DHCPv6 options specified in [RFC10038]: Distributing the Segment Routing over IPv6 (SRv6) Locator Using DHCPv6.¶

Table 112
IANA option code IANA option name
149 OPTION_IA_SRV6_LOCATOR
150 OPTION_IALOCATOR 
module: ietf-dhcpv6-opt-srv6-locator
  augment /dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/dhc6-srv:option-sets/
         dhc6-srv:option-set:
    +--rw ia-srv6-locator-option!
       +--rw iaid? uint32
       +--rw locator* inet:ipv6-prefix
    +--rw ialocator-option!
       +--rw locator? inet:ipv6-prefix
       +--rw preferred-lifetime? uint32
       +--rw valid-lifetime? uint32
¶ 
<CODE BEGINS> file "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-srv6-locator.yang"

module ietf-dhcpv6-opt-srv6-locator {
  yang-version 1.1;
  namespace "urn:ietf:params:xml:ns:yang:"
    + "ietf-dhcpv6-opt-srv6-locator";
  prefix srv6-loc;

  import ietf-inet-types {
    prefix inet;
    reference
      "RFC 6991: Common YANG Data Types";
  }
  import ietf-dhcpv6-server {
    prefix dhc6-srv;
    reference
      "RFC 9243: A YANG Data Model for DHCPv6
       Configuration";
  }

  organization
    "IETF Dynamic Host Configuration (DHC) Working Group";
  contact
    "DHC Working Group
     WG Web: <https://datatracker.ietf.org/wg/dhc/>
     WG List: <mailto:dhcwg@ietf.org>";
  description
     "This module defines the DHCPv6 options from RFC 10038:
      Distributing the Segment Routing over IPv6 (SRv6) Locator Using
      DHCPv6.";
  reference "RFC 10038: Distributing the Segment Routing over IPv6
    (SRv6) Locator Using DHCPv6";

  revision 2026-11-19 {
    description "Initial revision.";
    reference "RFC 10038: Distributing the Segment Routing over IPv6
      (SRv6) Locator Using DHCPv6";
  }

  grouping ia-srv6-locator-option-group {
    description "OPTION_IA_SRV6_LOCATOR (149) Identity Association for
      SRv6 Locator Option.";
    reference "RFC 10038: Distributing the Segment Routing over IPv6
      (SRv6) Locator Using DHCPv6";
    container ia-srv6-locator-option {
      presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
      description "OPTION_IA_SRV6_LOCATOR (149) Identity Association
        for SRv6 Locator Option option.";
      leaf iaid {
        type uint32;
        description "Identity association identifier.";
      }
      leaf-list locator {
        type inet:ipv6-prefix;
        description "SRv6 locator.";
      }
    }
  }

  grouping ialocator-option-group {
    description "OPTION_IALOCATOR (150) SRv6 Locator Option.";
    reference "RFC 10038: Distributing the Segment Routing over IPv6
      (SRv6) Locator Using DHCPv6";
    container ialocator-option {
      presence "Configures this DHCPv6 option.";
      description "OPTION_IALOCATOR (150) SRv6 Locator Option option.";
      leaf locator {
        type inet:ipv6-prefix;
        description "SRv6 locator.";
      }
      leaf preferred-lifetime {
        type uint32;
        description "Preferred lifetime.";
      }
      leaf valid-lifetime {
        type uint32;
        description "Valid lifetime.";
      }
    }
  }

  augment
    "/dhc6-srv:dhcpv6-server/"
      + "dhc6-srv:option-sets/"
      + "dhc6-srv:option-set" {
    description "Add RFC 10038 options.";
    uses ia-srv6-locator-option-group;
    uses ialocator-option-group;
  }

}

1.113. Conventions and Definitions

The key words "MUST", "MUST NOT", "REQUIRED", "SHALL", "SHALL NOT", "SHOULD", "SHOULD NOT", "RECOMMENDED", "NOT RECOMMENDED", "MAY", and "OPTIONAL" in this document are to be interpreted as described in BCP 14 [RFC2119] [RFC8174] when, and only when, they appear in all capitals, as shown here.¶
